# Kissing configuration of 8358 points in R^18: Kravatskiy's bent-coset hexagon configuration, rebuilt from the recipe and data files
# of A. Kravatskiy, KissingNumbers, GitHub repository (alexlegeartis/KissingNumbers), commit 86b7de10, folder dim18-bent-hexagon.
# This is not a configuration of the D18 paper. It is shipped so that the comparison in the paper (contact count 1621638,
# 7398 points with their antipode present) can be checked. The repository gives no coordinate file, only a recipe and data.
# Coordinates: 16 head coordinates, then 2 tail coordinates. All vectors are scaled by 6 (squared norm 288) and the tail is
# rotated by 45 degrees, so that every coordinate lies in Z[sqrt3].
# Format. Lines starting with # are comments. A number is written as m, m*sqrt(3), sqrt(3), -sqrt(3), etc.
# Each line 'extra x_1,...,x_18' is a nonzero vector p; the configuration is the set of unit vectors p/|p|.
# Any two distinct points x, y satisfy x.y <= |x||y|/2.
dimension 18
